## Selection rules

A folder is listed only if every regular file beneath it (re-scanned live when the plan was built) is in MANIFEST.sha256, verified in Spaces (size + MD5 for all files, SHA-256 download for the sample), unchanged in size, and none of the following. Listed folders are maximal: when a folder has a blocking file, only its clean subfolders are listed.
- any file refused or excluded from upload (secrets, seed-pattern matches, wasm32v1-none, cargo intermediates)
- Stellar transaction artifacts: `*.xdr`, names containing `signed`, `send` tokens, `journal`, `command` (command packs), `approval`
- `.wasm` files whose sha256 appears in any build-hashes*.json (all worktrees), addresses.md, Agents.md, CLAUDE.md, research/, config/, contracts/ or the keeper configs (deployed/recorded code hashes)
- whole tx-artifact folders under target/: deployer-key-rotation-*, treasury-*, mainnet-*, depth-droplet-*, stellar-margin-*
- never in scope: loose files at target/ root, all of /Users/joshua/Peridot/keeper-pyusd-vaultb (backup-only, incl. keeper v5 state/journal), the in-use `.wt-cl-group/.../target/clg` (PIDs 74590/74595)

Files that blocked a parent folder (counts): approval=6, command-pack=432, excluded-or-refused=1135, journal=6, send-file=50, signed-file=2519, tx-artifact-dir=48, wasm-known-hash=1260, xdr=4240

Symlinks inside listed folders are removed as links only (`rm -rf` does not follow them); see SYMLINKS.tsv.
